SAIF · College Football · Week 13

Our numbers first. The market second.

November 24–28, 2026

Research model, not a proven edge. Edges are shrunk to 15% of the raw model gap and plays are locked at the first consensus line of the week. Injuries are not in the model — open a game’s injury list before you bet it.

This week’s plays

SAIF plays: games where the shrunk edge was 1.0+ points at the locked entry line (the first consensus line of the week), in kickoff order. Flat 1 unit each, graded at the locked ticket (best number at FanDuel, DraftKings or BetRivers at entry). QB news after entry does not cancel a bet. Tap a play to jump to its game.

    Gamma picks

    Paper / small stakes (about ¼ unit). Gamma edge 4+ points at the current line, Alpha on the same side, no QB1 out on our side, and the line not moved 2+ points against Gamma since entry. Play of the day = line steady or moving our way and no QB question; playable = QB already priced/questionable or the line drifting against us. Gamma loses to the closing line historically; the bet is that early lines move its way.

      Tracked band: Alpha edge 1.5–3

      Paper strategy, graded every Monday at the locked entry number, −110. P4 conf = both teams in the same Power 4 conference (the version that held up best in 2018–25 history). Started Week 11 at 5–14 (P4 conference 2–5). Not a fund play until the end-of-season review.

        About the models, data & caveats

        Availability snapshot: Nov 27, 2026 · 9:02 AM ET. Open "Injuries & availability" on each game for player reports. This is a saved snapshot, not live data; no injury adjustments have been applied to either model.

        Alpha · football_adjusted_no_margin_10 — frozen ridge-10 fit, 2022–2024. Exact reproduction of the live model; coefficients loaded from the saved fitted_model.json, not re-estimated.

        Beta · Alpha + signed tier_gap (strict P4/G5) — joint refit, ridge-10, same 2022–2024 window. tier_gap = +1 home P4/away G5, −1 home G5/away P4, 0 otherwise.

        Both models predict home scoring margin from football data alone, with no market inputs. The market spread enters only afterward, to measure the difference. Opponent-adjusted ratings refit on all completed 2026 FBS-vs-FBS games through Week 12. Only 0 of this week’s games carry a nonzero tier_gap (flagged on the card) — everywhere else Alpha and Beta differ only by ordinary refit noise.

        Track record. Alpha went 364–370–18 ATS (49.6%) in the 2025 retrospective test. Average margin error: 12.97 points versus 11.89 for the market. Live 2026 results are on the Model performance page.

        Alpha inputs: net PPA, success rate, explosiveness, points per opportunity, havoc, turnover margin, neutral site, rest difference, and two opponent-rating features (offense and defense differences), blended with a four-game prior weight against 2025 team profiles. Beta is a full joint refit of Alpha’s 10 features plus one signed tier_gap feature — not Alpha plus a fixed correction, so even tier_gap=0 games can show small differences.

        SAIF policy (from Week 11). Through Week 9 the model’s raw edge carried only about 15% real information about the final margin, and its one positive signal was the line moving toward it early in the week. So the board shows a shrunk edge (0.15 × the Alpha gap to the line), a play needs 1.0+ shrunk points (about 6.7 raw), and each game is decided once, at the first consensus line we see, which is timestamped and locked. A pick on a team already missing its starting QB at entry is skipped; later QB news does not cancel a locked bet. Beta is kept as a shadow: logged and graded, never a play.

        “Where the margin comes from” is the same linear math the model already uses — each feature’s standardized value times its fitted coefficient — broken apart per feature. Neither model sees market lines, totals, or moneylines. 2025 has been examined repeatedly, so it is not a pristine holdout.

        Line = current market spread, favorite shown with −. SAIF = the pick of record at the locked entry number (solid = shrunk edge 1.5+, outline = 1.0–1.5). Shrunk edges under 1.0 show as No play. Gamma (newer model trained on 2016–2025 CFBD data) and Beta are shadow models: shown and tracked, never a play.

        Lines as of Fri Nov 27, 9:03 AM ET — consensus across US sportsbooks for 66 of 69 games (The Odds API), CollegeFootballData for the rest. Lines move; check your book before betting.
